2122-0003 ResultLocked There might have been an attempt to mount the data distribution cache storage while data was being synchronized. 1.
Do not mount the data distribution cache storage while the application is performing immediate synchronization.
2.
Data distribution cache storage is sometimes locked due to background synchronization.
You can reduce the chance of the application being unexpectedly locked by calling nn::bcat::Initialize() right after the application has started.		 Data download has not been completed.

Please try again later.		 No se ha podido completar la descarga.

2122-0008 ResultInternetRequestNotAccepted No Internet usage request has been received. Confirm that the Internet usage request, made by the application prior to this error, was received.
If the prior Internet usage request was received, this error might have occurred because the wireless or wired connection was dropped immediately before the communication.
It is not possible to completely eliminate the chance of this error occurring.		 A communication error has occurred.

2122-1998 ResultHttpErrorInsufficientSocketBuffer An error where the system process cannot allocate a socket buffer for server communication.
This can occur when the buffer is exhausted, for example when communication processes are being run on the console by multiple system processes.
The message in the dialog box refers to a communication error for convenience.		 There is no way to avoid this error on the application side.
Try running the function again after waiting for other system processes to release the buffer.		 A server communication error has occurred.

2122-2403 ResultServerError403 An error occurred during communication with the BCAT server. This error occurs in the following situations.

1.
There is a conflict with the IP address-based access restrictions for the BCAT Management Tool in the dd1 or lp1 server environment to which the system is connected.
In this situation, add the global IP address of the system to the BCAT Management Tool's IP white list.

2.
There is a conflict with the test delivery list-based access restrictions for the BCAT Management Tool in the dd1 or lp1 server environment to which the system is connected.
In this situation, add the Switch system’s serial number to the test delivery list.

3.
An HTTP 403 error occurred in the communication path between the Switch system and the server.
In this situation, review the Internet connection environment.

4.
A Nintendo server administrator changed a setting that prohibits server access by Switch systems with system versions earlier than 7.0.0.
This setting differs depending on the server environment.		 A server communication error has occurred.

Please try again later.
